# Laura Gao values screener: summary

> This is a **values filter only**. It helps choose among stocks that already fit the team's financial plan. It never makes up for weak finances or high valuation, and it never outweighs safely funding Laura's fixed $50,000 yearly payments for 2033-2042.

Model: `claude-opus-5-5` · Sections read: Item 1 + Item 7 (targeted) · Final score = Creativity/5×35 + Community/5×30 + Cross-cultural/5×20 + Entrepreneurship/5×15 · `*` = coverage insufficient (the filing says too little to judge that theme) · Shortlist = top 30% of each sector (at least 2, ties included) · A theme rescored from a sustainability report = 10-K score + 0.5 × (report score − 10-K score)

### 1. Reddit (RDDT): 60.0/100 · shortlist
10-K filed 2026-02-06 (fiscal 2025) · source: 10-K

Scores: Creativity 3 (sufficient) · Community 4 (sufficient) · Cross-cultural 3 (sufficient) · Entrepreneurship 1 (sufficient) → **60.0**

- **Creativity: 3/5** (sufficient): Level 3: the platform enables user-created content and gives creative tools across every subreddit, but the filing frames the business as conversation, knowledge and advertising rather than creative work, so it is a real but not core creative line (torn with 4, picked lower).
  - Hosts user-made content in subreddits, including posts, links, images and videos: "Within subreddits, Redditors engage in active and in-depth conversations by sharing experiences, submitting links, uploading images and videos, and replying to one another in comment threads on any topic." (Item 1 > Overview: The Power of Reddit’s Communities)
  - Gives moderators creative design tools and gives developers an open API to build bots and features for their communities: "We give moderators the creative tools to design the look and feel of communities, and we give developers an open application programming interface (“API”) to build bots and create features that shape their communities, meaning what a community envisions for itself drives its unique development." (Item 1 > Flexible Canvas for Self- and Community-expression)
- **Community: 4/5** (sufficient): Level 4: a community platform for peer learning and knowledge-sharing with 121.4 million DAUq is a major business line, but revenue comes from advertising and content licensing (torn with 5, picked lower).
  - Runs a large online platform where users join and create communities to learn from one another and exchange knowledge [121.4 million daily active uniques (DAUq) in the three months ended December 31, 2025]: "Reddit is a global, digital city where anyone in the world can join a community to learn from one another, engage in authentic conversations, explore passions, research new hobbies, exchange goods and services, create new communities and experiences, share a few laughs, and find belonging." (Item 1 > Overview: The Power of Reddit’s Communities)
- **Cross-cultural: 3/5** (sufficient): Level 3: machine translation live in 35 languages with about one billion posts translated is customer access with substantial measured reach, plus ERGs without figures; not yet shown as a major business line (torn with 4, picked lower).
  - Machine translation of posts into many languages so non-English speakers can take part [35 languages; approximately one billion posts translated]: "As of December 31, 2025, machine translation is live in 35 languages and approximately one billion posts have been translated in various languages, including Spanish, French, Italian, German, Portuguese, Swedish, Dutch, and Filipino." (Item 1 > Our Strategy for Growing Users and Engagement > Grow Our International User Base)
  - Employee Resource Groups for veterans, BIPOC, LGBTQ+, employees of various abilities, women and allies: "Just as our users seek community to address needs around belonging, we cultivate that within our company via our Employee Resource Groups (ERGs)." (Item 1 > Building the Future of the Internet at Reddit)
- **Entrepreneurship: 1/5** (sufficient): Level 1: small- and mid-size businesses appear only as advertisers, and Reddit Pro is a general business-presence tool with no specific program aimed at founders' barriers.
  - Reddit Pro tools that help businesses build an organic presence on Reddit: "Reddit Pro offers tools for businesses to discover, join, and contribute to Reddit’s communities and conversations, building relationships with our platform that can evolve into advertising partnerships." (Item 1 > The Future of Our Platform and Monetization Strategies > Advertising > Acquire More Advertisers)
- AI notes: Item 7 (targeted) excerpt was empty, so scoring rests on Item 1 only. Mission language ('empower communities', 'human creativity') was not scored on its own. Greater accessibility for small- and medium-sized businesses through future automation is planned, not current. The verified-profiles feature is a limited test, and Max campaigns (beta, January 2026) fall after the fiscal year; neither was scored. Reddit Answers rolling out to non-English languages supports the translation evidence but was not listed separately. Subreddits like r/ lgbt are user-run communities, not company programs, so they were not scored. The international user share (57%, 68.9 million international DAUq) was not scored because international reach alone does not count.

### 2. Roku, Inc. (ROKU): 54.0/100 · shortlist
10-K filed 2026-02-13 (fiscal 2025) · source: 10-K

Scores: Creativity 4 (sufficient) · Community 2 (sufficient) · Cross-cultural 2 (sufficient) · Entrepreneurship 2 (sufficient) → **54.0**

- **Creativity: 4/5** (sufficient): Level 4: licensing, distributing and enabling monetization of film/TV content is a major part of the reported Platform segment, with a disclosed library of 80,000+ titles; it is not scored 5 because revenue rests mainly on advertising and devices rather than the creative work itself, and when torn the lower level applies.
  - Operates The Roku Channel, a free ad-supported service that licenses and distributes a large library of films and TV shows, including Roku Originals [80,000+ movies and TV shows]: "Viewers can stream a broad variety of 80,000+ movies and TV shows, including Roku Originals, for free." (Item 1 > Platform Segment > The Roku Channel)
  - Runs a streaming platform where content partners publish apps to distribute and monetize their streaming content: "Our significant scale, ability to reach highly engaged viewers, tools that enable seamless signups, and marketing/discovery features make us an attractive platform to content partners." (Item 1 > Platform Segment > Platform Revenue)
- **Community: 2/5** (sufficient): Level 2: a named external Social Impact education program and employee training are described, but no participant or dollar figures show substantial reach.
  - Social Impact program in which employees work with educational and workforce development organizations to give young people industry insight and hands-on experience: "Purposeful investments in developing future talent across our industry are made through our Social Impact program." (Item 1 > Human Capital Management)
  - Learning and Growth team provides onboarding, training, 1:1 leadership coaching and on-demand skill development for employees: "Our Learning and Growth team provides our employees with the training and development needed to support our strategic priorities and growth." (Item 1 > Human Capital Management)
- **Cross-cultural: 2/5** (sufficient): Level 2: employee resource groups supported by an Inclusion Strategy team, with no membership figures; international expansion is excluded by the rubric.
  - Inclusion Strategy team supports employee resource groups and inclusion programs: "Our Inclusion Strategy team partners across Roku to advance inclusion through strategic programs, support our employee resource groups, expand talent communities, and amplify our employer brand story." (Item 1 > Human Capital Management)
- **Entrepreneurship: 2/5** (sufficient): Level 2: a specific Roku Ads Manager integration lowers TV-advertising production costs for small and medium-sized businesses, with no disclosed reach.
  - Roku Ads Manager partner integration that lets small and medium-sized businesses repurpose social media content for TV ads, lowering production cost: "We also have a partner integration with Roku Ads Manager to enable SMBs to efficiently repurpose social media content for TV streaming, reducing production costs and complexity to make advertising on TV more accessible." (Item 1 > Business Growth > Advertising Innovation)
- AI notes: The Item 7 (targeted) section was empty, so all evidence comes from Item 1. Several items were left out under the rubric's traps or limits. 'Entrepreneurial, execution-focused culture' is a culture claim and scores at most 1. Partnerships with Shopify and others on action ads are advertiser tools, not small-business enabling. Roku City 'original storytelling' is an ad product, which falls under the brand-marketing storytelling trap. 'Inclusive content' and storytelling that represents audiences from different backgrounds is general language with no specific program. The Platform segment's reach (more than 90 million Streaming Households, 145.6 billion Streaming Hours) is disclosed in other paragraphs, so it supports the Creativity level 4 judgment but is not cited as an indicator. Howdy (nearly 10,000 hours) and Frndly TV (more than 50 live channels) are further owned content-distribution services under the same Creativity activity and were not recorded separately.

### 3. Omnicom Group (OMC): 40.0/100
10-K filed 2026-02-20 (fiscal 2025) · source: 10-K

Scores: Creativity 3 (sufficient) · Community 2 (sufficient) · Cross-cultural 1 (sufficient) · Entrepreneurship 1 (sufficient) → **40.0**

- **Creativity: 3/5** (sufficient): Level 3: creative advertising and content production through named agency networks is a substantial, current activity, but the provided text discloses no creative-specific revenue, users or customers, so level 4 is not met (lower level chosen when torn).
  - Operates named creative advertising agency brands that produce original creative advertising work for clients: "OA includes our creative brands, BBDO, TBWA, and McCann, which we acquired from IPG, and the brands included within the Advertising Collective." (Item 1 > Our Business)
  - Pays third-party artists and freelance creative talent when producing client advertising: "Third-party direct costs incurred in connection with the creation and delivery of advertising, marketing, and communications services include, among others: purchased media, studio production services, specialized talent, including artists and other freelance labor, event marketing supplies, materials and services, promotional items, market research and third-party data and other related expenditures." (Item 7 > Principal vs. Agent)
- **Community: 2/5** (sufficient): Level 2: only employee training and development is described, so the employee-development cap of 2 applies.
  - Training and development programs for employees' leadership and professional skills: "Recognizing the importance of this core competency, we support and develop our employees through training and development programs that build and strengthen employees’ leadership and professional skills." (Item 1 > Human Capital Resources and Environmental Sustainability Initiatives)
- **Cross-cultural: 1/5** (sufficient): Level 1: rests only on general language (a commitment to an inclusive work environment), with no specific inclusion program or practice described.
- **Entrepreneurship: 1/5** (sufficient): Level 1: rests only on general language about acquiring companies with entrepreneurial management teams, with no service enabling founders or small businesses.
- AI notes: Final score: 21 + 12 + 4 + 3 = 40/100. Creativity: advertising is client-commissioned brand marketing. It was counted as 'creates' because the agencies produce creative services, content and studio production. This is a judgment call against the 'storytelling as brand marketing' trap and may merit human review. Cross-cultural: 'multi-cultural marketing' appears only as an undescribed item in a list of services, so it was not scored as a specific activity. Coverage: the human capital section runs about 280 words, so Community and Cross-cultural were marked sufficient, but the inclusion content within it is a single sentence. 'Education | 1 | %' in the Item 7 excerpt appears to be client-industry revenue share, not an education activity, and was not counted. The MD&A text is targeted excerpts only. The merger with IPG closed on November 26, 2025, so IPG operations are included only from that date.

### 4. Match Group (MTCH): 25.0/100
10-K filed 2026-02-26 (fiscal 2025) · source: 10-K

Scores: Creativity 1 (sufficient) · Community 1 (sufficient) · Cross-cultural 3 (sufficient) · Entrepreneurship 0 (sufficient) → **25.0**

- **Creativity: 1/5** (sufficient): Level 1: the only creative activity is marketing content and influencer partnerships, which are a routine business input, and the core dating and social connection apps neither create nor enable creative work.
  - Creates marketing content and partners with influencers to promote its dating apps: "Our online marketing activities generally consist of purchasing social media advertising, advertising on streaming services, banner, and other display advertising, search engine marketing, email campaigns, video advertising, business development or partnership arrangements, creating content, and partnering with influencers, among other means to promote our services." (Item 1 > Sales and marketing)
- **Community: 1/5** (sufficient): Level 1: the human capital section is over 150 words but describes only generic, unnamed employee learning programs; with no external education or community investment disclosed, the score falls between levels 1 and 2 and the lower level is chosen.
  - Employee talent, learning and development programs: "Our talent, learning and development programs provide employees with resources to help achieve their career goals, build strong foundational technical and leadership skills, and contribute to and, where applicable, lead their organizations." (Item 1 > Human capital)
- **Cross-cultural: 3/5** (sufficient): Level 3: multiple named, ongoing affinity brands serve communities defined by shared culture or experiences and are positioned by management as one of three strategic portfolio dimensions (Familiarity), plus Azar connects users across the globe in their native language; no measured reach is disclosed, so level 4 is not met.
  - Operates a set of named affinity-based dating brands serving communities defined by shared culture, values or experiences: "BLK®, Chispa®, Upward®, Salams®, HER®, Archer®, Yuzu®, The League®, and other affinity-based brands, serve communities defined by shared culture, values, or experiences." (Item 1 > Our portfolio > Evergreen & Emerging ("E&E"))
  - Azar video chat service lets users meet people across the globe in their native language: "Azar is a one-to-one video chat service that allows users to meet and interact with a variety of people across the globe in their native language." (Item 1 > Our portfolio > Match Group Asia ("MG Asia"))
- **Entrepreneurship: 0/5** (sufficient): Level 0: the business description is a consumer dating and social connection service with no small-business customers or enabling programs; mentions of innovation and empowering internal brand leaders are traps.
- AI notes: The Item 7 targeted excerpt was empty, so no MD&A evidence was available. The Familiarity strategic dimension ('serving communities defined by shared values, culture, or experiences') describes the same affinity brands as E2, so it supports E2's significance and is not counted separately. Azar was removed from the Apple App Store on February 22, 2026, after the fiscal year. It remains available on the web and Google Play, so it is still treated as current. Pairs is 'designed to address social barriers' around dating-app stigma in Japan; this is not treated as cross-cultural inclusion because operating in a foreign market does not count. Tinder serving 18–30 year-olds who were 'historically underserved' is about age and is not counted. 'Communities of users' and 'sense of community' describe a dating user base, not the education or collaboration that the Community theme asks for, so they are not scored there. Hinge Video and Voice Prompts are user self-presentation features, not creative work. Headcount fell about 12% in 2025 due to a cost-reduction initiative. No coverage is insufficient, so no second look is triggered.

### 1. Etsy (ETSY): 80.0/100 · shortlist
10-K filed 2026-02-19 (fiscal 2025) · source: 10-K

Scores: Creativity 5 (sufficient) · Community 3 (sufficient) · Cross-cultural 3 (sufficient) · Entrepreneurship 5 (sufficient) → **80.0**

- **Creativity: 5/5** (sufficient): Level 5: enabling independent creators and artisans to sell creative goods is the core business (Etsy marketplace is 87.8% of GMS).
  - Operates the Etsy marketplace, where independent sellers and artisans sell unique, creative and handmade goods [Etsy marketplace GMS $10,460.7 million or 87.8% of the total]: "The Etsy marketplace is the global destination for unique, creative goods from independent sellers, connecting artisans with thoughtful consumers seeking items that reflect their tastes and values." (Item 1 > Overview > About our Company)
- **Community: 3/5** (sufficient): Level 3: community investment with substantial disclosed reach (Uplift Fund reaching nearly 7,690 entrepreneurs and $1.9 million in grants, about $3.4 million in donations), plus employee development and limited external education.
  - Uplift Fund supports organizations that give creative entrepreneurs capital and business support, with buyer round-up donations at checkout [nearly 7,690 entrepreneurs; 4.4 million times; $1.9 million in grants]: "In 2025, our Uplift Fund supported organizations providing creative entrepreneurs with capital and wrap-around business support services for nearly 7,690 entrepreneurs." (Item 1 > Our Impact Strategy, Goals, & Progress > Social > Creative Community)
  - Philanthropic donations to community-based organizations, plus an Impact Investment Fund [$30 million Impact Investment Fund; approximately $3.4 million in philanthropic donations]: "For example, our $30 million Impact Investment Fund makes investments or provides loans to initiatives that are aligned with our Impact strategy, and, in 2025, Etsy made approximately $3.4 million in philanthropic donations to community-based organizations doing work that supports our Impact goals." (Item 1 > Our Impact Strategy, Goals, & Progress > Values-Aligned Investment)
- **Cross-cultural: 3/5** (sufficient): Level 3: specific current inclusion practice with a substantial disclosed figure ($39 million spent with diverse-owned suppliers), alongside ERGs and disability-inclusive workplace practices.
  - Supplier diversity spending with women-, disability-, LGBTQ- and minority-owned suppliers [$39 million]: "During 2025, we spent $39 million with diverse-owned suppliers, which we define as woman, disability, and LGBTQ-owned globally; as well as Black, Asian-Indian, Asian-Pacific, Hispanic/Latinx, Indigenous, and veteran-owned in the United States." (Item 1 > Our Impact Strategy, Goals, & Progress > Social > Supply Chain > Supplier Diversity)
  - Employee Resource Groups across Etsy and Depop: "And our Employee Resource Groups (“ERGs”) continue to thrive across Etsy and Depop, contributing to a culture of inclusion across our brands." (Item 1 > Our Impact Strategy, Goals, & Progress > Social > Workforce Access > Ensuring Equal Access to Employment)
- **Entrepreneurship: 5/5** (sufficient): Level 5: seller marketplaces that let independent creators and small businesses start and grow are the core business, supported by enabling programs such as the Uplift Fund.
  - Operates the Etsy marketplace, where independent sellers and artisans sell unique, creative and handmade goods [Etsy marketplace GMS $10,460.7 million or 87.8% of the total]: "The Etsy marketplace is the global destination for unique, creative goods from independent sellers, connecting artisans with thoughtful consumers seeking items that reflect their tastes and values." (Item 1 > Overview > About our Company)
  - Uplift Fund supports organizations that give creative entrepreneurs capital and business support, with buyer round-up donations at checkout [nearly 7,690 entrepreneurs; 4.4 million times; $1.9 million in grants]: "In 2025, our Uplift Fund supported organizations providing creative entrepreneurs with capital and wrap-around business support services for nearly 7,690 entrepreneurs." (Item 1 > Our Impact Strategy, Goals, & Progress > Social > Creative Community)
- AI notes: Depop (E9) operated through FY2025, but on February 15, 2026 Etsy agreed to sell it to eBay (expected close Q2 2026), and it will become a discontinued operation. Its loss would not change any theme score. Reverb (musical instrument marketplace) was sold June 2, 2025 and Elo7 in 2023; both are past and not scored. Active Etsy sellers declined year over year after the 2024 seller set-up fee. Seller Census figures (e.g., 89% businesses of one, 59% sold for the first time on Etsy) come from a 2024 survey and were not used as indicators. The CERF+ $2,000 disaster grants to sellers, fee deferrals for nearly 77,000 sellers, and a pilot grant to the River Arts District (over 550 artists) are current activities that support Creativity/Community/Entrepreneurship. They were not recorded as evidence because their only complete-sentence quotes are well over 40 words, and they would not change any score. E3's $3.4 million in donations and $30 million Impact Investment Fund may overlap with E2 (Uplift Fund grants) and E4/E5 (Impact Investment Fund loans), so watch for double counting. Etsy Insider loyalty program is a beta. The Item 7 excerpt only repeats the Item 1 overview sentence. Cross-cultural was scored 3 based mainly on the supplier diversity dollar figure; ERGs alone would score 2.

### 2. Duolingo (DUOL): 63.0/100 · shortlist
10-K filed 2026-02-27 (fiscal 2025) · source: 10-K

Scores: Creativity 2 (sufficient) · Community 5 (sufficient) · Cross-cultural 4 (sufficient) · Entrepreneurship 1 (sufficient) → **63.0**

- **Creativity: 2/5** (sufficient): Level 2: a specific current arts-education offering (the music course) with no separate reach figures, while art and animation are otherwise a routine product input.
  - Music course teaching learners to read music notes and play songs: "In 2023, we launched a music course that's designed to teach music literacy, including how to read music notes and play songs." (Item 1 > Our Business)
  - In-house character art, animation and backstories used in lessons: "Our cheerful characters, with their own personalities and backstories, show up throughout the courses and make the learning experience more interesting and meaningful." (Item 1 > The Duolingo Method)
- **Community: 5/5** (sufficient): Level 5: education and learning is the core business, shown by the language app with more than 130 million MAUs, the English Test, and the schools and literacy products.
  - Free-to-use mobile language-learning app offering hundreds of language courses to a global user base [over 250 total language courses; more than 130 million monthly active users]: "As of December 31, 2025, Duolingo offers over 250 total language courses to more than 130 million monthly active users." (Item 1 > Our Business)
  - Duolingo English Test, a low-cost online alternative to test-center English proficiency exams for international student admissions [over 6,100 education programs accept results; over 5,300 of those being higher education programs]: "By offering a more accessible, online option that is both rigorous and accurate, we provide greater opportunities for aspiring students and professionals dependent on the successful completion of these high stakes assessments." (Item 1 > Our Business)
- **Cross-cultural: 4/5** (sufficient): Level 4: customer access across languages is a major part of the business with measured reach (over 250 language courses, more than 130 million MAUs, and an accessible English test accepted by over 6,100 programs).
  - Free-to-use mobile language-learning app offering hundreds of language courses to a global user base [over 250 total language courses; more than 130 million monthly active users]: "As of December 31, 2025, Duolingo offers over 250 total language courses to more than 130 million monthly active users." (Item 1 > Our Business)
  - Duolingo English Test, a low-cost online alternative to test-center English proficiency exams for international student admissions [over 6,100 education programs accept results; over 5,300 of those being higher education programs]: "By offering a more accessible, online option that is both rigorous and accurate, we provide greater opportunities for aspiring students and professionals dependent on the successful completion of these high stakes assessments." (Item 1 > Our Business)
- **Entrepreneurship: 1/5** (sufficient): Level 1: the filing shows only the company's own innovation and no service enabling founders or small businesses.
  - Ongoing patent program protecting the company's own assessment innovations [two patent applications]: "Duolingo has filed two patent applications directed to proprietary techniques we developed for generating language proficiency test questions and evaluating language proficiency as part of an on-going program to identify and protect innovations developed by the Company." (Item 1 > Intellectual Property)
- AI notes: Mission and values language (for example, 'make it universally available' and the company principles) was not scored above 1. Cross-cultural was weighed between 4 and 5: language learning removes language barriers, and the filing notes refugees among learners and more Irish and Hawaiian learners than native speakers. However, the core business is framed as education, so the lower level of 4 was chosen. The People and Culture section has only an inclusion statement, a legal-compliance hiring policy, and workforce background (E7, demographics only), with no employee networks or programs. No employee training programs are described. Free access and the freemium model are part of the same app (E1) and are not counted separately. The music course has no separate user figures. Math and Chess courses are covered under E1. The Item 7 excerpt on Duolingo English Test acceptance is truncated mid-sentence, so Item 1 was used for quotes. No small-business or founder-enabling activity is disclosed.

### 3. Life Time Group Holdings, Inc. (LTH): 36.0/100 · shortlist
10-K filed 2026-02-24 (fiscal 2025) · source: 10-K

Scores: Creativity 1 (sufficient) · Community 3 (sufficient) · Cross-cultural 2 (sufficient) · Entrepreneurship 1 (sufficient) → **36.0**

- **Creativity: 1/5** (sufficient): Level 1: creative work appears only as a routine business input (in-house architecture and design of the company's own centers); the core business is fitness clubs, not creating or enabling creative work.
  - In-house architecture and design team designs the company's own fitness centers: "We also benefit from our in-house architecture and design expertise that allows us to create operationally efficient centers and a consistent feel across our centers." (Item 1 > Our Growth Strategies and Member Experience Initiatives > Flexible Asset-light Real Estate Model)
- **Community: 3/5** (sufficient): Level 3: the named Life Time Foundation is a community investment program with dollar and reach figures (more than $12 million since 2010, millions of children), supported by employee development and an external certification academy.
  - Life Time Foundation funds youth nutrition and youth movement programs in schools and communities [more than $12 million in support since 2010; millions of children]: "Since 2010, the Life Time Foundation has provided more than $12 million in support to communities nationwide and positively impacted millions of children." (Item 1 > Life Time Foundation)
  - Life Time Education, an internal certification, learning and development division for all team members: "LTE delivers certification, learning, education and development opportunities for all team members." (Item 1 > Human Capital > Development and Training)
- **Cross-cultural: 2/5** (sufficient): Level 2: specific current internal practices (Inclusion Council and five employee resource groups) with no membership or reach figures.
  - Life Time Inclusion Council with ambassadors at each center works on inclusion and cultural competency: "Our Inclusion Council works through committees to identify and incubate areas for enhancing inclusion and cultural competency within our organization." (Item 1 > Human Capital > Inclusion at Life Time)
  - Employee resource groups open to all team members [five employee resource groups]: "Further, team members are encouraged to find support and resources in one or more of our five employee resource groups, which are open to all team members." (Item 1 > Human Capital > Inclusion at Life Time)
- **Entrepreneurship: 1/5** (sufficient): Level 1: rests on general language only (an 'entrepreneurial spirit' and the company's own innovation), with no specific service enabling founders or small businesses.
- AI notes: The Foundation's $12 million figure is cumulative since 2010, not annual. The Foundation's 2023 expansion into forestation and conservation is environmental and not scored. Life Time Academy is described inside team-member training, so whether outsiders enroll is not entirely clear, and no enrollment figures are given; it is tagged external_education at level 2 only, and Community level 3 rests on E2. Life Time Foundation and LTA are the only external activities; the rest of Community evidence is employee development. Kids Academy, swim lessons and kids camps are fee-based member services with no figures and were not scored separately. 'Charity Events' appears only as a table entry with no description. The 51,800 member events reflect member social activity, not external education. Traps excluded: 'state-of-the-art' equipment; 'studio performers' (fitness instructors, not artists); Experience Life and digital 'content' (health and fitness content in general, not described as creative work); 'entrepreneurial spirit'; 'accessibility laws' (regulatory compliance only); member demographics (income, education, age) are a disclosure, not inclusion activity; 'diversified portfolio' of centers. Life Time Work co-working is not described as serving founders or small businesses. Item 7 excerpts repeat Item 1 content and add no new qualifying activities.

### 4. Airbnb (ABNB): 33.0/100 · shortlist
10-K filed 2026-02-12 (fiscal 2025) · source: 10-K

Scores: Creativity 1 (sufficient) · Community 1 (sufficient) · Cross-cultural 2 (sufficient) · Entrepreneurship 4 (sufficient) → **33.0**

- **Creativity: 1/5** (sufficient): Level 1: the business description shows no creating or enabling of creative work, and the cultural-institution collaborations are described only as brand marketing.
  - Brand sponsorships and co-marketing collaborations with cultural institutions and media platforms: "We also leverage select sponsorships and co‑marketing collaborations with cultural institutions, sports and entertainment organizations, destinations, and media platforms to amplify our brand, align with key cultural moments, and engage our community globally." (Item 1 > Our Marketing)
- **Community: 1/5** (sufficient): Level 1: the roughly 230-word human capital section offers only general language about employee learning programs, with no specific employee, external education or community investment activity.

(Item 1 > Human Capital Management > Community)

In Item 7, 'food accessibility' appears only in a regulatory risk list and is not a program. Some activities were mentioned but not recorded separately. The donation of a roughly 19,000 sq ft building and over 30 acres to the Akron Canton Regional Foodbank has no stated date, and that partner is already within E1. Milk-Bone funding for Canine Assistants is also covered by E1. Inclusive benefits (domestic partner insurance coverage, a floating holiday for personal beliefs, an annual pay equity analysis) and tuition assistance would only reinforce levels already reached. The statement about a workforce that 'reflects the consumers we serve' is general language. The 10-K points to the 2025 Corporate Impact Report, but coverage is sufficient because the 10-K itself describes specific activities.

### 1. Adobe Inc. (ADBE): 58.0/100 · shortlist
10-K filed 2026-01-15 (fiscal 2025) · source: 10-K

Scores: Creativity 5 (sufficient) · Community 2 (sufficient) · Cross-cultural 2 (sufficient) · Entrepreneurship 1 (sufficient) → **58.0**

- **Creativity: 5/5** (sufficient): Level 5: selling tools that enable others' creative work (Creative Cloud, Express, Stock, Firefly) is the core business described throughout Item 1.
  - Sells Creative Cloud Pro, a subscription suite of creative apps (Photoshop, Illustrator, Premiere Pro, After Effects and others) for creative professionals and enthusiasts: "Adobe Creative Cloud Pro is a cloud-based subscription to an ecosystem of apps that enables creative professionals and enthusiasts alike to express themselves and collaborate with apps and services for photography, design, video, social media, and more that connect across surfaces, platforms and geographies." (Item 1 > Principal Solutions > Creative & Marketing Professionals > Adobe Creative Cloud Pro)
  - Runs Adobe Stock, a library of licensed creative assets (photos, illustrations, video, audio, 3D) built into its apps [millions]: "Adobe Stock provides designers and businesses with access to millions of high-quality, curated, royalty-free photos, vectors, illustrations, videos, templates, audio and 3D assets, for all their creative projects." (Item 1 > Principal Solutions > Creative & Marketing Professionals > Adobe Stock)
- **Community: 2/5** (sufficient): Level 2: the only qualifying activity is employee development (Learning Fund), so the score is capped at 2.
  - Learning Fund that pays employees toward university and short-term learning [$11,000 per year]: "Through Adobe’s Learning Fund, employees are eligible to receive up to $11,000 per year toward university and short-term learning opportunities." (Item 1 > Human Capital > Growth and Development)
- **Cross-cultural: 2/5** (sufficient): Level 2: the filing describes Employee Networks but gives no membership or reach figures.
  - Employee Networks and community-building forums for employees: "We also strive to build community by bringing together our employees through onsite events, discussion groups, messaging forums and our Employee Networks as we believe that a sense of belonging contributes to our employees’ overall wellbeing." (Item 1 > Human Capital > Compensation, Benefits and Wellbeing)
- **Entrepreneurship: 1/5** (sufficient): Level 1: solopreneurs and small business owners are named as customers, but no specific enabling program for founders is described.
  - Digital Media segment customers include solopreneurs and small business owners: "Our customers span creative professionals, including graphic designers, photographers, videographers, illustrators and 3D artists; creators, including social media influencers and solopreneurs; business professionals, including social media teams, small business owners and knowledge workers; and consumers." (Item 7 > Digital Media)
- AI notes: Creativity was scored 5 because the filing presents creative tools as the core business. Acrobat (document productivity) and the Digital Experience segment (marketing and data) are large non-creative parts of the business, and no segment revenue split was provided. Without revenue figures, someone could argue for 4. Digital Learning Solutions (free and fee-based training and certifications on Adobe products) was treated as customer product training, not external education. Adobe Research's 'collaboration with universities' is a research partnership and was not counted. 'Charitable contributions' appears only as a G&A expense line with no program or amount. Adobe Ventures is corporate venture capital described as strategic investment, so it was not counted as an enabling program. 'Adobe for All' is a cultural label, and the Employee Networks are not described by purpose or membership. 'Accessible entry points' (freemium pricing) and international field offices were not counted for Cross-cultural. A board seat at StoryCorps and the Fine Arts Museums of San Francisco is an executive's personal activity, not company activity. Segments will be combined into one starting in fiscal 2026. All quotes use the filing's curly apostrophes.

10-K-only scores before the report: {'creativity': 3, 'community': 2, 'cross_cultural': 1, 'entrepreneurship': 2} → 43.0
If the report counted in full: {'creativity': 3, 'community': 2, 'cross_cultural': 2, 'entrepreneurship': 2} → 47.0

- **Creativity: 3/5** (sufficient): Level 3: enabling creative work through named creator-facing product lines (Professional Visualization GPUs, Omniverse, GeForce for creators) is a real but minor part of the business; no creative-specific revenue, users or customers are disclosed, so level 4 is not met.
  - Sells workstation GPUs and software (Professional Visualization market) that artists use for creative work such as film visual effects: "Professional artists, architects and designers use NVIDIA partner products accelerated with our GPUs and software platform for a range of creative, engineering, and design use cases, such as creating visual effects in movies or designing buildings and products." (Item 1 > Our Company)
  - Omniverse 3D design collaboration software for artists, designers and creators: "Omniverse is real-time 3D design collaboration and virtual world simulation software that empowers artists, designers, and creators to connect and collaborate in leading design applications." (Item 1 > Business Strategies > Extending our technology and platform leadership in computer graphics)
- **Community: 2/5** (sufficient): Level 2: employee development plus a named external training program (Deep Learning Institute) with no participant or dollar figures, so substantial external reach for level 3 is not shown.
  - Deep Learning Institute provides in-person and online AI training to external developers worldwide: "Our Deep Learning Institute provides in-person and online training for developers in industries and organizations around the world to build AI and accelerated computing applications that leverage our platforms." (Item 1 > Sales and Marketing)
  - Employee development through on-the-job training and tuition reimbursement: "We invest in employee development through on-the-job trainings and tuition reimbursement programs." (Item 1 > Human Capital Management)
- **Cross-cultural: 1.5/5** (sufficient) (10-K 1, report 2; report counted at 0.5 weight): Level 2: The report describes specific current internal practices. These are 10 named community resource groups open to all employees (including LGBTQ+, disability, Asian Pacific Islander and other groups), a global allyship training program, CRG mentoring, and an inclusive-practices guide for managers. No CRG membership figures are disclosed. The only reach figure is CRG mentoring for about 1,200 employees, which is modest against roughly 42,000 staff. There is no customer-access or connecting-communities activity. Torn between 2 and 3, so the lower level applies.
  - Ten community resource groups open to all employees, including groups for Arab, Asian Pacific Islander, Black, Hispanic-Latino, LGBTQ+, disabled, veteran, South Asian and women employees [10 community resource groups (no membership figures disclosed)]: "We support 10 community resource groups (CRGs), which are open to all employees: Arabs & Allies Network, Asian Pacific Islanders & Allies, Black NVIDIAN Network, Early Career Network, Hispanic-Latino Network, NV Pride (LGBTQ+ employees and allies), NVIDIA Disabilities Network, Veterans@NVIDIA, South Asians & Allies, and Women in Technology." (Our People > Community Resource Groups, sustainability report)
  - Employee-run global allyship program that trains staff to support peers: "The global program trains NVIDIANs to show up as allies for their peers, leading to a sense of belonging across the company." (Our People > Allyship, sustainability report)
- **Entrepreneurship: 2/5** (sufficient): Level 2: a named startup program (Inception) reaching tens of thousands of startups, but the filing does not say what enabling support it provides, so between levels 2 and 3 the lower level is chosen.
  - Inception program working with startups [tens of thousands of startups]: "We evangelize AI through partnerships with hundreds of universities and tens of thousands of startups through our Inception program." (Item 1 > Business Strategies > Extending our technology and platform leadership in AI)
- AI notes: Cross-cultural coverage is insufficient, so a second look using the sustainability report is triggered. The Item 7 excerpt only discusses open-source foundation models and has no qualifying activity. Excluded as traps: 'foundation' models and 'foundational' CUDA; 'training' meaning AI model training; chip 'design' and co-design; IP 'license and development agreements'; supply chain and sales in Asia and the Middle East; 'innovation' language. The Deep Learning Institute and the university partnerships appear under Sales and Marketing and AI evangelism, which is close to the 'educating customers as marketing' trap, so DLI was given no more than level 2 and the 'hundreds of universities' partnerships were not scored separately. The Graphics segment (GeForce and RTX workstation GPUs) is a reported segment, but no revenue is given in the provided text and it is mostly gaming, which is consumption rather than creation, so Creativity is not scored at 4. The 7.5 million CUDA developers relate to NVIDIA's own platform ecosystem and were not counted for Entrepreneurship.

XBOX also enables third-party developers and 'new content creators' through Game Pass and digital stores; this was not recorded separately to avoid double counting within Creativity, and it was judged too nonspecific for Entrepreneurship. (Item 7 > Community Outreach)

(Item 7 > Community Outreach)
  - Employment, vocational and productive-skills training for people in mining communities, including training local small and medium supplier businesses [1,219 people, 829 people in employment, 390 people in regional vocational and productive skills, 368 local businesses]: "In relation to economic development, we trained 1,219 people in mining communities in 2025, including 829 people in employment, 390 people in regional vocational and productive skills and 368 local businesses to support the development of small and medium mining suppliers." (Item 1 > Human Capital Resources > 2026 Strategic Workplace Plan)
- **Entrepreneurship: 2/5** (sufficient): Level 2: specific current training for 368 local small and medium supplier businesses, which is not a named program or a business line.

E3 supports Community (training for community members) and Entrepreneurship (supplier business development) because it independently meets each theme's criteria. E5 (Works for Taxes projects) may overlap with the $25.2 million social infrastructure figure in E4; it is listed separately because it is a distinct mechanism, but it could be double counting. Training of 1,400 leaders under the zero-tolerance harassment policy was treated as policy enforcement, not a cross-cultural activity. The Great Place to Work awards, including the Best Workplaces for Women ranking, are recognitions, not activities. The 2026 Strategic Workplace Plan (E11), including workplace accessibility modifications, is planned and does not count. The Tía María social strategy's 'productive development' initiatives and the Michiquillay 'business opportunities' are general or planned, so they were not scored for Entrepreneurship. Social programs at Los Chancas are described only vaguely and the project is stalled by illegal miners. Traps excluded: 'state of the art mining', 'foundation works' (construction), 'investment community'. Townsite schools are employee housing amenities and were not scored separately.
